$ ["eval", "() => { const s=getComputedStyle(document.querySelector(\"aside button.MuiButton-contained\")); return {color:s.color,background:s.backgroundColor}; }"]

}
### Ran Playwright code
```js
await page.evaluate('() => { const s=getComputedStyle(document.querySelector("aside button.MuiButton-contained")); return {color:s.color,background:s.backgroundColor}; }');
```
